// Model Definitions
// Defines each model used by the game or mod
// Format:
// # Indicates start of an entry followed by the file path.  ./ is assumed
// example: Models/my_super_awesome_model.obj
// Acceptable file formats: .obj
// The path must include the file extension
// 
// Next are key:value pairs separated by : and whitespace ignored
// Acceptable keys: index
// index indicates which index into the master model array to use.
// All other systems reference what model to use by index (e.g. entity 
// definition for a pickup item will specify model by index)
// example: index: 451
